are some orthogonal
matrices such that
\bm{Z}_{2}^{\top}\bm{Z}_{3}=\bm{U}^{\star\top}\bm{Z}_{2}=\bm{U}^{\star\top}\bm{Z}_{3}=\bm{0}_{r\times r}
.
It is straightforward to verify that
\|K^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\|=1-\theta
.

For the loading matrices, we let
\bm{V}^{\star}
be an arbitrary orthogonal matrix and
\bm{W}^{\star}

be
\bm{W}^{\star}=0.6\bm{V}^{\star}+0.8\bm{Z}_{1}
, where
\bm{Z}_{1}

is some orthogonal matrix such that
\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{Z}_{1}=\bm{0}
.
This construction makes
\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{W}^{\star}=0.6\bm{I}_{r}
. For all

k\in[K]
, we set
\bm{V}_{k}^{\star}=\sigma_{\min}\bm{V}^{\star}/\sqrt{0.4}

and
\bm{W}_{k}^{\star}=\sigma_{\min}\bm{W}^{\star}/\sqrt{0.4}
.
It can be verified that
\sigma_{2r}(\bm{A}_{k}^{\star})=\sigma_{\min}.

From this point forward, we assume without loss of generality that
\sigma_{\min}=\sqrt{0.4}
,
so that
\bm{V}_{k}^{\star}=\bm{V}^{\star}
and

\bm{W}_{k}^{\star}=\bm{W}^{\star}
for all
k\in[K]
.

Identifying
\bm{Q}
.

We establish a fourth-order approximation for
\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}\widehat{\bm{W}}_{k}^{\top}

by utilizing the expansion presented in
[
Xia21
]
(see Section
E.1

for a brief introduction).

Lemma 11
.

Suppose
\rho\leq C_{1}

for some small enough constant
C_{1}
. Then with probability at
least
1-O(KN^{-10})
,

\bm{A}_{k}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}\widehat{\bm{W}}_{k}^{\top}=\bm{T}_{0,k}+\bm{T}_{1,k}+\bm{T}_{2,k}+\bm{T}_{3,k}+\bm{T}_{4,k}+O(\rho^{-5}),

(64)

where
\bm{T}_{i,k}
is an
i
-th degree polynomial of the noise
matrix
\bm{E}_{k}
and
\|\bm{T}_{i,k}\|=O(\rho^{-i})
.

The proof of this lemma is deferred to Section
E.2
. Using this lemma, we can see that for each
k\in[K]
,

\left(\bm{A}_{k}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}\widehat{\bm{W}}_{k}^{\top}\right)\left(\bm{A}_{k}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}\widehat{\bm{W}}_{k}^{\top}\right)^{\top}=\sum_{i=0}^{4}\sum_{j=0}^{4-i}\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}+O(\rho^{-5}).

(65)

We may now define the fourth-order approximation to be

\bm{Q}\coloneqq\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{i=0}^{4}\sum_{j=0}^{4-i}\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}.

(66)

From (
65
) and (
12
), we deduce
that
\bm{M}=\bm{Q}+O(\rho^{-5})
.

Biased estimation of
\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}
.

We characterize what SVD would achieve on the expectation
of
\bm{Q}
. We start with a precise characterization of
\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}

in the following lemma. The proof is deferred to Section
F
.

Lemma 12
.

Instate the assumptions of Theorem
6
.
Let
\bm{Q}
be defined as in (
66
). Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}

\displaystyle=(1+\alpha_{1})\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}+\alpha_{2}\bm{I}_{n}+\alpha_{3}\cdot\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}

(67)

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha_{4}\cdot\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}+\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right),

where
\{\alpha_{i}\}_{i=1}^{4}
are real numbers satisfying
|\alpha_{i}|\leq C_{1}\rho^{-2}

for
i=1,2,3
and
\alpha_{4}\in[C_{2}\sigma^{4}nd,C_{3}\sigma^{4}nd]

for some constants
C_{1},C_{2},C_{3}>0
and
C_{2}<C_{3}
.

The cross term
\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}

is simplified from
\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}
.
By picking
\bm{V}^{\star},\bm{W}^{\star}
not orthogonal to each other, we introduce a non-trivial
cross term that leads to biased estimation. Let
\widetilde{\bm{U}}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}

be the matrix whose columns are the top-
r
eigenvectors of

\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}
. The following lemma formally
demonstrates this induced bias.

Lemma 13
.

Instate the assumptions of Theorem
6
.
Let
\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}
be the eigen space
of
\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}
and
\alpha_{1},\alpha_{4}
be the scalars
appeared in Lemma
12
. Then

\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}=\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}+\frac{\alpha_{4}}{1+\alpha_{1}}\cdot\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}+\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right)+O(\rho^{-6}).

Proximity of
\bm{M}
and
\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}
.

The following lemma shows that
\bm{M}
is close to
\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}
.

Lemma 14
.

Instate the assumptions of Theorem
6
,
with probability at least
1-O(KN^{-10})
,

\left\|\bm{M}-\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}\right\|\leq C_{1}\left[\frac{\log N}{\sqrt{K}}\cdot\rho^{-1}+\rho^{-5}\right]

for some constant
C_{1}>0
.

Proof of the lower bound (
63
).

We assume that the event that Lemma
11
,

13
, and
14
hold, which happens
with probability at least
1-O(KN^{-10})
. We first bound
\|\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}\|

and then
\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\|
.

By Wedin’s theorem,

\left\|\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}\right\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\left\|\bm{M}-\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}\right\|}{\sigma_{r}(\mathbb{E}\bm{Q})-\sigma_{r+1}(\mathbb{E}\bm{Q})-\left\|\bm{M}-\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}\right\|}.

(68)

Take
\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}

as the ground truth matrix and
\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}

as the perturbation. By Weyl’s inequality and (
67
),

\displaystyle\sigma_{r}(\mathbb{E}\bm{Q})-\sigma_{r+1}(\mathbb{E}\bm{Q})

\displaystyle\geq 1-2\left\|\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\geq 1-2\left(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}+2\alpha_{4}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{4},

where the last line holds as long as
\rho\geq C_{1}
for
some large enough constant
C_{1}
. More over, by assumption of Theorem
6
,

K\geq C_{1}\log^{2}N
and
\rho\geq C_{2}
for some large
enough constant
C_{1},C_{2}
. Then

\|\bm{M}-\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}\|\leq C_{1}\left[\frac{\log N}{\sqrt{K}}\cdot\rho^{-1}+\rho^{-5}\right]\leq\frac{1}{8}+\frac{1}{8}\leq 1/4.

Applying these bounds and Lemma
14
to (
68
),
we have

\left\|\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}\right\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\left\|\bm{M}-\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}\right\|}{1/4}\leq C_{3}\left[\frac{\log N}{\sqrt{K}}\cdot\rho^{-1}+\rho^{-5}\right]

for some constant
C_{3}>0
,

On the other hand, by Lemma
13
,

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\geq\left\|\frac{\alpha_{4}}{1+\alpha_{1}}\cdot\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}+\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right)\right\|-O(\rho^{-6}).

We lower bound the main term:

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}+\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\geq\sup_{\bm{v}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}:\|\bm{v}\|\leq 1}\left\|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}+\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right)\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{v}\right\|

\displaystyle=\sup_{\bm{v}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}:\|\bm{v}\|\leq 1}\left\|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{v}\right\|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(i)}}{=}\sup_{\bm{v}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}:\|\bm{v}\|\leq 1}\left\|\sqrt{1-\theta}\bm{Z}_{2}\bm{v}\right\|

\displaystyle=\sqrt{1-\theta}.

Here (i) follows from the definition of
\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}
.
Recall that
|\alpha_{1}|\leq C_{4}\rho^{-2}
and
|\alpha_{4}|\geq C_{5}\sigma^{4}nd

for some constants
C_{4},C_{5}
. Assuming
\theta\leq 1/2
and
\rho\geq C_{2}

for some large enough constant
C_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\alpha_{4}}{1+\alpha_{1}}\sqrt{1-\theta}-O(\rho^{-6})

\displaystyle\geq C_{6}\sigma^{4}nd-O(\rho^{-6})

for some constant
C_{6}>0
.

Combining the lower bound of
\|\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}\|

and
\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\|
,
we conclude that

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\geq\left\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right\|-\left\|\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\geq C_{6}\sigma^{4}nd-O(\rho^{-6})-C_{3}\left[\frac{\log N}{\sqrt{K}}\cdot\rho^{-1}+\rho^{-5}\right]

\displaystyle\geq C_{6}\sigma^{4}nd-C_{3}\left[\frac{\log N}{\sqrt{K}}\cdot\rho^{-1}+\rho^{-5}\right]

as long as
\rho\geq C_{2}
for some large enough constant

C_{2}
.

E.1
Approximation of SVD

In this section, we give a brief explanation of the
approximation of SVD in
[
Xia21
]
. This is based on Theorem 1
and Section 3 of
[
Xia21
]
Here we introduce it for asymmetric
matrices. The symmetric version is more striaightforward since Theorem 1
in
[
Xia21
]
would be applicable. All the notations in this
section works as a generic result and is not related to our problem
setting.

Let
\bm{A}=\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{Y}^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}

be a rank-
r
matrix and its SVD. Let

\bm{P}^{-1}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{0}&\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}\bm{Y}^{\top}\\
\bm{Y}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}&\bm{0}\end{bmatrix},\qquad\text{and}\qquad\bm{P}^{\perp}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{I}-\bm{X}\bm{X}^{\top}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{I}-\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\end{bmatrix}.

Overloading the notation we also use
\bm{P}^{0}
to denote
\bm{P}^{\perp}
.
Furthermore

\bm{P}^{-2k}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}^{-2k}\bm{X}^{\top}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{Y}\bm{\Sigma}^{-2k}\bm{Y}^{\top}\end{bmatrix},\qquad\text{and}\qquad\bm{P}^{-2k-1}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{0}&\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}^{-2k-1}\bm{Y}^{\top}\\
\bm{Y}\bm{\Sigma}^{-2k-1}\bm{X}^{\top}&\bm{0}\end{bmatrix}

for any integer
k
. Let
\bm{E}
be error matrix and

\widetilde{\bm{E}}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{0}&\bm{E}\\
\bm{E}^{\top}&\bm{0}\end{bmatrix}

be its dilation. Then let
\widehat{\bm{X}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\widehat{\bm{Y}}^{\top}

be the SVD of
\bm{M}+\bm{E}
, we have that
\mathcal{S}_{i}

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\bm{X}}\widehat{\bm{X}}^{\top}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\widehat{\bm{Y}}\widehat{\bm{Y}}^{\top}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{X}\bm{X}^{\top}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\end{bmatrix}+\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}\mathcal{S}_{i}

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{\alpha}\in\mathbb{N}^{i+1},\sum_{j=1}^{i+1}\alpha_{j}=i}(-1)^{|\{j:\alpha_{j}\neq 0\}|+1}\bm{P}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}\bm{P}^{-\alpha_{2}}\ldots\bm{P}^{-\alpha_{i}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}\bm{P}^{-\alpha_{i+1}}.

In addition, it can be computed that

\|\mathcal{S}_{i}\|\leq\left(\frac{4\|\bm{E}\|}{\lambda_{r}(\bm{A})}\right)^{i}.

E.2
Proof of Lemma
11

In this section we give an overview of the fourth order expansion
of
\bm{A}_{k}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}\widehat{\bm{W}}_{k}^{\top}
.
To apply the analysis in Section
E.1
, we define
the following matrices. Let

\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\coloneqq\begin{bmatrix}\bm{0}&\bm{E}_{k}\\
\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}&\bm{0}\end{bmatrix}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\bm{P}_{k}^{0}\coloneqq\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{I}-\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{I}-\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\end{bmatrix}.

By Theorem 4.4.5 in
[
Ver18
]
, as long as
\log N\leq C_{1}N

for some constant
C_{1}
, with probability at least
1-N^{-10}
,

\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\|=\|\bm{E}_{k}\|\leq C_{2}\sigma\sqrt{n\vee d_{k}}\leq C_{2}\sigma\sqrt{N}=O(\rho^{-1})

for some large enough constant
C_{2}>0
. For any positive integer

s
, let

\bm{P}_{k}^{-2s}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\end{bmatrix}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\bm{P}_{k}^{-2s-1}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{0}&\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\\
\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}&\bm{0}\end{bmatrix}.

Note that
\|\bm{P}_{k}^{-2s}\|=\|\bm{P}_{k}^{-2s-1}\|=1
. Using
the approximation of SVD in
[
Xia21
]
we described in Section
E.1

on the ground truth matrix
\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

and its perturbation
\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{A}_{k}=\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}+\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}
,
we have that
\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}^{\top}=\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k}^{\top}+O(\rho^{-5})

for

\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}^{\top}=\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}+\sum_{i=1}^{4}\bm{N}_{i,k}+O(\rho^{-5})

where

\bm{N}_{i,k}\coloneqq\left[\sum_{\bm{\alpha}\in\mathbb{N}^{i+1},\sum_{j=1}^{i+1}\alpha_{j}=i}(-1)^{|\{j:\alpha_{j}\neq 0\}|+1}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\ldots\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{i}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{i+1}}\right]_{1:n,1:n}.

(69)

Since
\|\bm{P}_{k}^{-2s}\|=\|\bm{P}_{k}^{-2s-1}\|=1
,
\|\bm{N}_{i,k}\|\leq\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\|=O(\rho^{-i})
.
Now consider
\bm{A}_{k}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}\widehat{\bm{W}}_{k}^{\top}

. We use the approximation to reach that

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{k}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}\widehat{\bm{W}}_{k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}_{k}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{A}_{k}

\displaystyle=\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}+\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}+\bm{E}_{k}-\left(\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}+\sum_{i=1}^{4}\bm{N}_{i,k}\right)\left(\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}+\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\right)+O(\rho^{-5}).

Renaming the terms with their respective degrees (number of
\bm{E}_{k}
),
we can write this approximation as

\bm{A}_{k}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}\widehat{\bm{W}}_{k}^{\top}=\bm{T}_{0,k}+\bm{T}_{1,k}+\bm{T}_{2,k}+\bm{T}_{3,k}+\bm{T}_{4,k}+O(\rho^{-5}),

where

\displaystyle\bm{T}_{0,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\bm{T}_{1,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}-\bm{N}_{1,k}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\bm{T}_{2,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\bm{N}_{1,k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}-\bm{N}_{2,k}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

(70)

\displaystyle\bm{T}_{3,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\bm{N}_{2,k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}-\bm{N}_{3,k}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\bm{T}_{4,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\bm{N}_{3,k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}-\bm{N}_{4,k}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

Note that the term
-\bm{N}_{4,k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}

is hidden in the residual term
O(\rho^{-5})
. Moreover we
have that
\|\bm{T}_{i,k}\|=O(\rho^{-i})
.

E.3
Proof of Lemma
13

Lemma
12
tells us that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}

\displaystyle=(1+\alpha_{1})\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}+\alpha_{2}\bm{I}_{n}+\alpha_{3}\cdot\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha_{4}\cdot\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}+\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right),

where
\{\alpha_{i}\}_{i=1}^{4}
are real numbers such that
|\alpha_{i}|\leq C_{1}\sigma^{2}(\sqrt{nd}+n)

for
i=1,2,3
and
\alpha_{4}\in[C_{2}\sigma^{4}nd,C_{3}\sigma^{4}nd]

for some constants
C_{1},C_{2},C_{3}>0
and
C_{2}<C_{3}
. Treat

(1+\alpha_{1})\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}
as the ground truth
and

\displaystyle\bm{E}_{U}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\alpha_{2}\bm{I}_{n}+\alpha_{3}\cdot\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}+\alpha_{4}\cdot\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}+\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right)

as the perturbation. It is easy to see that
\|\bm{E}_{U}\|\leq C_{3}\rho^{-2}\leq 1/8

as long as
\rho^{-1}\leq C_{4}
for some large enough constant

C_{3},C_{4}>0
. Moreover, for
j=1,2
, let
\bm{P}^{-j}\coloneqq(1+\alpha_{1})^{-j}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}

and
\bm{P}^{\perp}\coloneqq\bm{I}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}
.
Now we may apply Theorem 1 in
[
Xia21
]
to get that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}+\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{\perp}+\bm{P}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{-1}

(71)

\displaystyle\quad+\bm{P}^{-2}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{\perp}+\bm{P}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{-2}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{\perp}+\bm{P}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{-2}

\displaystyle\quad-\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{\perp}-\bm{P}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{-1}-\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{-1}+O(\|\bm{E}_{U}\|^{3}).

Observe that for
j=1,2
,

\left\|\bm{P}^{-j}\bm{E}_{U}\bm{P}^{\perp}\right\|=\left\|\frac{\alpha_{4}}{(1+\alpha_{1})^{j}}\cdot\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\right\|\leq\alpha_{4}.

Then we can simplify the expansion (
71
) as

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}+\frac{\alpha_{4}}{1+\alpha_{1}}\cdot\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}+\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right)+O(\alpha_{4}\|\bm{E}_{U}\|)+O(\|\bm{E}_{U}\|^{3})

\displaystyle=\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}+\frac{\alpha_{4}}{1+\alpha_{1}}\cdot\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}+\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right)+O\left(\rho^{-6}\right).

The proof is now completed.

E.4
Proof of Lemma
14

Recall that

\displaystyle\bm{M}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\bm{A}_{k}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}\widehat{\bm{W}}_{k}^{\top}\right)\left(\bm{A}_{k}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{k}\widehat{\bm{W}}_{k}^{\top}\right)^{\top}.

\displaystyle=\bm{Q}+O(\rho^{-5}),

where

\bm{Q}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{i=0}^{4}\sum_{j=0}^{4-i}\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}.

It suffices to show
\|\bm{Q}-\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}\|=O(1/\sqrt{K})
.
The matrix
\bm{Q}
is a sum of independent random matrices
\sum_{i=0}^{4}\sum_{j=0}^{4-i}\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}
,
so we may use the matrix Bernstein inequality. We claim that for each

k\in[K]
, as long as
N\geq C_{1}\log N
for some large enough constant

C_{1}>0
, with probability at least
1-O(N^{-11})
,

\left\|\sum_{i=0}^{4}\sum_{j=0}^{4-i}\left(\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}\right)\right\|\leq C_{2}\rho^{-1};

(72a)

and

\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{i=0}^{4}\sum_{j=0}^{4-i}\left(\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}\right);\left\|\sum_{i=0}^{4}\sum_{j=0}^{4-i}\left(\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}\right)\right\|>C_{2}\rho^{-1}\right\|\leq C_{2}\rho^{-1}N^{-11}.

(72b)

for some constant
C_{2}>0
. Moreover,

\left\|\mathbb{E}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[\sum_{i=0}^{4}\sum_{j=0}^{4-i}\left(\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}\right)\right]\left[\sum_{i=0}^{4}\sum_{j=0}^{4-i}\left(\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}\right)\right]^{\top}\right\|\leq C_{2}^{2}K\cdot\rho^{-2}.

(72c)

and Now invoking the truncated matrix Bernstein
inequality (see
[
CCFM21
]
, Corollary 3.2), we have
that with probability at least
1-O(KN^{-10})
,

\|\bm{Q}-\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}\|\leq\frac{C_{3}\log N}{\sqrt{K}}\cdot\rho^{-1}

for some large enough constant
C_{3}>0
.

We now prove (
72a
) to (
72c
). It
is easy to see that (
72c
) is a direct consequence
of (
72a
). It now suffices to show (
72a
).
By Theorem 4.4.5 in
[
Ver18
]
, as long as
\log N\leq C_{1}N

for some constant
C_{1}
, with probability at least
1-N^{-11}
,

\|\bm{E}_{k}\|\leq C_{4}\sigma\sqrt{n\vee d_{k}}\leq C_{4}\sigma\sqrt{N}=C_{4}\rho^{-1}.

Since
\bm{T}_{0,k}=\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}
is not random,

\sum_{i=0}^{4}\sum_{j=0}^{4-i}\left(\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}\right)=\sum_{(i,j):1\leq i+j\leq 4}\left(\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}\right).

From the form of
\bm{T}_{i,k}
we discussed in Section
F.1
,
we observe that

\|\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}\|\leq\|\bm{E}_{k}\|^{i+j}\leq(C_{4}\rho^{-1})^{i+j}\leq C_{4}\cdot\rho^{-1}

as long as
\rho\geq 1/C_{4}
. Therefore

\left\|\sum_{i=0}^{4}\sum_{j=0}^{4-i}\left(\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}\right)\right\|\leq C_{2}\cdot\rho^{-1}

for some constant
C_{2}>0
.

Finally (
72b
) can be proved similar to (
23a
).
We omit it here for conciseness.

Appendix F
Proof of Lemma
12

In this section we give the proof of Lemma
12
. In particular, we compute
\mathbb{E}\bm{Q}

by brute force, i.e., we calculate the expectation of all possible monomial and
sum them up. In Section
F.1
we exhaust all
monomials that shows up in
\bm{T}_{i,k}
for
i=0,1,2,3,4
and

k\in[K]
. Then in Section
B
,
we provide some useful results that assist our computation. For conciseness
in the notation, we define the projections
\mathcal{P}_{U}\coloneqq\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}
,

\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\coloneqq\bm{I}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}
,

\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}=\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}
,
\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=\bm{I}-\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}
,

\mathcal{P}_{W}=\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}
, and
\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}=\bm{I}-\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}
.

Recall that

\bm{Q}\coloneqq\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{i=0}^{4}\sum_{j=0}^{4-i}\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}.

where
\bm{T}_{0,k}
is a sum of
i
-th degree monomials of
\bm{E}_{k}
.
For
i=j=0
, it is easy to see that
\bm{T}_{0,k}\bm{T}_{0,k}^{\top}=\bm{S}_{0}\bm{S}_{0}^{\top}=\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}
.
When
i+j
is odd,
\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}
is the sum
of several odd degree monomial of
\bm{E}_{k}
. Therefore its expectation
is 0. Thus we only need to focus on the terms where
i+j
is even.

In the rest of this section, we will first write out the expression
of each term. We consider the second-order terms (
i+j=2
) and fourth-order
terms (
i+j=4
) separately. For the second-order terms, the following
lemma describes their expectation. The proof is deferred to Section
F.2
.

Lemma 15
.

Let
\bm{T}_{i,k}
be defined
as in (
70
) for
i=0,1,2
and
k\in[K]
. We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{1,k}\bm{T}_{1,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}\left(n\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}-r_{k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\right)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{0,k}\bm{T}_{2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=0.

Similarly we can have a similar result for the fourth-order terms
in the following Lemma. The proof is deferred to Section
F.3
.

Lemma 16
.

Let
\bm{T}_{i,k}
be defined
as in (
70
) for
i=0,1,2,3,4
and
k\in[K]
. We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{2,k}\bm{T}_{2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}\left[\left(2n-r-2r_{k}\right)r_{k}\bm{I}-\left(2n-r-2r_{k}\right)r_{k}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\left(n^{2}-nr-4nr_{k}+2rr_{k}+3r_{k}^{2}\right)\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\right]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{1,k}\bm{T}_{3,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-2\sigma^{4}(dr-r^{2})\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{0,k}\bm{T}_{4,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=0.6\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})(d-r_{k})\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}.

Combining these terms, as long as
\sigma^{2}(\sqrt{nd}+n)\leq C_{1}

for some small enough constant
C_{1}
and
r\leq(n\wedge d)/3
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\sum_{i=0}^{4}\sum_{j=0}^{4-i}\bm{T}_{i,k}\bm{T}_{j,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=(1+\alpha_{1})\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}+\alpha_{2}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}+\alpha_{3}(\bm{I}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top})

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha_{4}\left(\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}+\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right),

where
\{\alpha_{i}\}_{i=1,2,3,4}
are some scalars such that
|\alpha_{i}|\leq C_{1}\sigma^{2}(\sqrt{nd}+n)

for
i=1,2,3
and
\alpha_{4}\in[C_{2}\sigma^{4}nd,C_{3}\sigma^{4}nd]

for some constants
C_{1},C_{2},C_{3}>0
and
C_{2}<C_{3}
, given
that Summing it up over
k\in[K]
gives us (
67
). Note that
we assume
r_{k}=r
for all
k\in[K]
.

F.1
Decomposition of (
64
) into monomials

In this Section we write down all the hidden monomials (
64
)
to facilitate further computation. For each
\bm{T}_{i,k}
, we write
it as a summation

\bm{T}_{i,k}=\sum_{j}\bm{S}_{i,j,k},

where each
\bm{S}_{i,j,k}
is an
i
-th degree monomial of
\bm{E}_{k}
.
In what follows, we list all such monomials grouped by their degree.

F.1.1
First-order terms

From (
70
), we have that

\displaystyle\bm{T}_{1,k}

\displaystyle=\bm{S}_{1,1,k}+\bm{S}_{1,2,k}.

(73)

We then define

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{1,1,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k};

(74)

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{1,2,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\bm{N}_{1,k}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=-\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

F.1.2
Second-order terms

Recall from (
70
) that

\bm{T}_{2,k}=-\bm{N}_{1,k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}-\bm{N}_{2,k}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

We compute each term separately. For the first term
-\bm{N}_{1,k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}
,

-\bm{N}_{1,k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bm{S}_{2,i,k},

where

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{2,1,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k};

(75)

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{2,2,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}.

For the second term
-\bm{N}_{2,k}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}
,
each monomial can be written as

-(-1)^{|\{i:\alpha_{i}>1\}|+1}\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{3}}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

as long as
\sum_{i}\alpha_{i}=2
. When
\alpha_{3}=0
,

\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{3}}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\right]_{1:n,1:n}(\bm{I}-\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top})\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}_{n\times d}.

Thus we only need to consider the cases where
\alpha_{3}\neq 0
.
We then have

-\bm{N}_{2,k}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\sum_{j=3}^{5}\bm{S}_{2,j,k},

where

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{2,3,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-2}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=-\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top},

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{2,4,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top},

(76)

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{2,5,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

F.1.3
Third-order terms

Recall from (
70
) that

\bm{T}_{3,k}=-\bm{N}_{2,k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}-\bm{N}_{3,k}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

For the first term
-\bm{N}_{2,k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}
,

-\bm{N}_{2,k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=\sum_{j=1}^{6}\bm{S}_{3,j,k},

(77)

where

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,1,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-2}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=-\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,2,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-2}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=-\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,3,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-2}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=-\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,4,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,5,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,6,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}.

For the second term
-\bm{N}_{3,k}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}
,
each monomial can be written in the form of

-(-1)^{|\{i:\alpha_{i}>1\}|+1}\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{3}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{4}}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

for some
\bm{\alpha}\in\mathbb{N}^{4}
such that
\sum_{i}\alpha_{i}=3
.
If
\alpha_{4}=0
,

\displaystyle\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{3}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{4}}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\quad=\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{3}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\right]_{1:n,1:n}(\bm{I}-\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top})\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=0.

We consider the cases when
\alpha_{4}\neq 0
. We have that

-\bm{N}_{3,k}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\sum_{j=7}^{16}\bm{S}_{3,j,k},

(78)

where
\bm{S}_{3,j,k}
as defined below. For readability we group
the terms by the multiset (unordered set allowing repetition) of
\{\alpha_{i}\}_{i=1}^{4}
.

Term for
\{\alpha_{i}\}=\{0,0,0,3\}
:

\bm{S}_{3,7,k}\coloneqq-\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-3}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=-\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

Terms for
\{\alpha_{i}\}=\{0,0,1,2\}
:

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,8,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-2}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top};

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,9,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-2}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top};

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,10,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-2}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top};

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,11,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-2}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top};

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,12,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-2}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top};

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,13,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-2}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

Terms for
\{\alpha_{i}\}=\{0,1,1,1\}:

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,14,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=-\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top};

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,15,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=-\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top};

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,16,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-1}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=-\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

F.1.4
Fourth-order terms

Recall from (
70
) that

\bm{T}_{4,k}=-\bm{N}_{3,k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}-\bm{N}_{4}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

For the first term
-\bm{N}_{3,k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}
,
each monomial can be written as

-(-1)^{|\{i:\alpha_{i}>1\}|+1}\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{3}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{4}}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k},

for some
\bm{\alpha}\in\mathbb{N}^{4}
such that
\sum_{i}\alpha_{i}=3
.
We then have

-\bm{N}_{3,k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=\sum_{j=1}^{20}\bm{S}_{4,j,k},

where
\bm{S}_{4,j,k}
as defined below. For readability we group
the terms by the multiset (unordered set allowing repetition) of
\{\alpha_{i}\}_{i=1}^{4}
.

Terms for
\{\alpha_{i}\}=\{0,0,0,3\}:

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{4,1,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-3}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=-\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{4,2,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-3}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{\perp}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=-\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{4,3,k}

For the second term
-\bm{N}_{4}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}
,
each monomial can be written as

-(-1)^{|\{i:\alpha_{i}>1\}|+1}\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{3}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{4}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{5}}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top},

for some
\bm{\alpha}\in\mathbb{N}^{5}
such that
\sum_{i}\alpha_{i}=4
.
If
\alpha_{5}=0
,

\displaystyle\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{3}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{4}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{5}}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\quad=\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{3}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{4}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\right]_{1:n,1:n}(\bm{I}-\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top})\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=0.

Thus we focus on the cases when
\alpha_{5}\neq 0
. We then have

-\bm{N}_{4,k}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\sum_{j=21}^{55}\bm{S}_{4,j,k},

where
\bm{S}_{4,j,k}
as defined below. For readability we group
the terms by the multiset (set allowing repeation) of
\{\alpha_{i}\}_{i=1}^{4}
.
We also discuss the case when
\alpha_{5}=1
separately.

Terms for
\{\alpha_{i}\}=\{0,0,0,0,4\}
:

, we have that

\displaystyle\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{3}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{4}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{5}}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\quad=\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{3}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{4}}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\quad=\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{3}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{4}}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

Now observe that since
\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}+\alpha_{4}=3
,

-(-1)^{|\{i:\alpha_{i}>1\}|+1}\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{3}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{4}}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{S}_{4,j,k}

for some
j\in[20]
. We can then write
\bm{S}_{4,j,k}
as

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{4,j,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-(-1)^{|\{i:\alpha_{i}>1\}|+1}\left[\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{2}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{3}}\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{k}\bm{P}_{k}^{-\alpha_{4}}\right]_{1:n,1:n}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

(79)

\displaystyle=-\bm{S}_{4,j-35,k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

for any
j=36,\ldots,55
. This result will be convenient for our
computation of the expectation.

F.2
Proof of Lemma
15

There are two possible configurations of the second-order terms in
the expectation:
\bm{T}_{0,k}\bm{T}_{2,k}^{\top}
(and its transpose)
and
\bm{T}_{1,k}\bm{T}_{1,k}^{\top}
. We consider each of them separately.
We have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{1,k}\bm{T}_{1,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}\left(n\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}-r_{k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\right);

(80a)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{0,k}\bm{T}_{2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=0.

(80b)

We defer the proof of (
80a
) to Section
F.2.1

and the proof of (
80b
) to Section
F.2.2
.

F.2.1
Proof of (
80a
)

Combining (
73
), (
74
), and Lemma
1
,
we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{1,1,k}\bm{S}_{1,1,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{E}_{k}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\right]\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=n\sigma^{2}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{1,1,k}\bm{S}_{1,2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\right]\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=-r_{k}\sigma^{2}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{1,2,k}\bm{S}_{1,2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\right]\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=r_{k}\sigma^{2}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}.

Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{1,k}\bm{T}_{1,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{1,1,k}\bm{S}_{1,1,k}^{\top}+\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{1,1,k}\bm{S}_{1,2,k}^{\top}+\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{1,2,k}\bm{S}_{1,1,k}^{\top}+\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{1,2,k}\bm{S}_{1,2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}\left(n\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}-r_{k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\right).

F.2.2
Proof of (
80b
)

Since
\bm{T}_{0,k}
is a non-random quantity, it suffices to compute

\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{2,k}
. Recall that
\bm{T}_{0,k}=\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}

and
\bm{T}_{2,k}=\sum_{j=1}^{5}\bm{S}_{2,j,k}
(see Section
F.1.2
).
We use (
75
), (
76
), and Lemma
1

to see that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,1,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=-(n-r-r_{k})\sigma^{2}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,2,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}=0

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,3,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=0

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,4,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=0

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,5,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=(n-r-r_{k})\sigma^{2}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

Summing these up, we have that

\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{0,k}\bm{T}_{2,k}^{\top}=\bm{T}_{0,k}\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{2,k}^{\top}=0.

F.3
Proof of Lemma
16

There are three possible configurations of the fourth order terms:

\bm{T}_{0,k}\bm{T}_{4,k}^{\top}
,
\bm{T}_{1,k}\bm{T}_{3,k}^{\top}
,
and
\bm{T}_{2,k}\bm{T}_{2,k}^{\top}
(and the corresponding transposes).
We will show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{2,k}\bm{T}_{2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}\left[\left(2dr-3r^{2}\right)\bm{I}-\left(2dr-3r^{2}\right)\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}+\left(nd-4dr-nr+5r^{2}\right)\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\right]

(81a)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{1,k}\bm{T}_{3,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-2\sigma^{4}(dr-r^{2})\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

(81b)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{0,k}\bm{T}_{4,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})(d-r_{k})\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}.

(81c)

Then the proof of Lemma
16
is
completed by substituting
\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{W}^{\star}=0.6\bm{I}
.
We defer the proof of (
81a
), (
81b
), and (
81c
)
to Section
F.3.1
,
F.3.2
,
and
F.3.3
, respectively. To be concise, in the
rest of this section, we compute the expectations with Lemma
2

without explicitly mentioning the lemma.

F.3.1
Proof of (
81a
)

Recall that
\bm{T}_{2,k}=\sum_{j=1}^{5}\bm{S}_{2,j,k}
(see Section
F.1.2
).
For the symmetric terms in the form of
\bm{S}_{2,j,k}\bm{S}_{2,j,k}^{\top}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,1,k}\bm{S}_{2,1,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\right]\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})(n+d-r-r_{k}+1)\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,2,k}\bm{S}_{2,2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\right]\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}dr_{k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,3,k}\bm{S}_{2,3,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}(d-r-r_{k})r_{k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,4,k}\bm{S}_{2,4,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}r_{k}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,5,k}\bm{S}_{2,5,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})(n-r+1)\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}.

For the asymmetric terms,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,1,k}\bm{S}_{2,2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\right]\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,1,k}\bm{S}_{2,3,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,1,k}\bm{S}_{2,4,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,1,k}\bm{S}_{2,5,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle=-\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})(n-r+1)\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,2,k}\bm{S}_{2,3,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,2,k}\bm{S}_{2,4,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=-\sigma^{4}r_{k}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,2,k}\bm{S}_{2,5,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,3,k}\bm{S}_{2,4,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,3,k}\bm{S}_{2,5,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{2,4,k}\bm{S}_{2,5,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}.

Combining all these terms and the fact that
r=r_{k}
(note that
cross terms need to include their transpose), we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{2,k}\bm{T}_{2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{5}\sum_{j=1}^{5}\bm{S}_{2,i,k}\bm{S}_{2,j,k}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}\left[\left(2dr-3r^{2}\right)\bm{I}-\left(2dr-3r^{2}\right)\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}+\left(nd-4dr-nr+5r^{2}\right)\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\right].

F.3.2
Proof of (
81b
)

Recall that
\bm{T}_{1,k}=\sum_{j=1}^{2}\bm{S}_{1,j,k}
,
\bm{T}_{3,k}=\sum_{j=1}^{16}\bm{S}_{3,j,k}

(see Section
F.1.1
and
F.1.3
). For each term

\bm{S}_{1,i,k}\bm{S}_{3,j,k}^{\top}
, we first will discuss the
case of
j=1,\ldots,6
and
j=7,\ldots,16
separately.

We start with the case
j=1,\ldots,6
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,1,k}\bm{S}_{1,1,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,1,k}\bm{S}_{1,2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,2,k}\bm{S}_{1,1,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=-\sigma^{4}(n+d-r-r_{k}+1)r_{k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,2,k}\bm{S}_{1,2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}(n-r+1)r_{k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,3,k}\bm{S}_{1,1,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=-\sigma^{4}(d-r_{k})r_{k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,3,k}\bm{S}_{1,2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,4,k}\bm{S}_{1,1,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}r_{k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,4,k}\bm{S}_{1,2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=-\sigma^{4}r_{k}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,5,k}\bm{S}_{1,1,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,5,k}\bm{S}_{1,2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,6,k}\bm{S}_{1,1,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,6,k}\bm{S}_{1,2,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}=\bm{0}

Now we discuss the case of
j=7,\ldots,16
. All the terms in this
category ends with
\bm{W}^{\star\top}
. This is convenient for us
since then

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{3,j,k}(\bm{S}_{1,1,k}+\bm{S}_{1,2,k})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\bm{S}_{3,j,k}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}-\bm{S}_{3,j,k}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=\bm{S}_{3,j,k}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}.

We can use it to compute that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,7,k}(\bm{S}_{1,1,k}+\bm{S}_{1,2,k})^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,8,k}(\bm{S}_{1,1,k}+\bm{S}_{1,2,k})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,9,k}(\bm{S}_{1,1,k}+\bm{S}_{1,2,k})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,10,k}(\bm{S}_{1,1,k}+\bm{S}_{1,2,k})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,11,k}(\bm{S}_{1,1,k}+\bm{S}_{1,2,k})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,12,k}(\bm{S}_{1,1,k}+\bm{S}_{1,2,k})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,13,k}(\bm{S}_{1,1,k}+\bm{S}_{1,2,k})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,14,k}(\bm{S}_{1,1,k}+\bm{S}_{1,2,k})^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,15,k}(\bm{S}_{1,1,k}+\bm{S}_{1,2,k})^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{3,16,k}(\bm{S}_{1,1,k}+\bm{S}_{1,2,k})^{\top}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}.

Combining all these terms and the fact that
r_{k}=r
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{1,k}\bm{T}_{3,k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{5}\sum_{j=1}^{5}\bm{S}_{1,i,k}\bm{S}_{3,j,k}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=-2\sigma^{4}(dr-r^{2})\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}.

F.3.3
Proof of (
81c
)

As
\bm{T}_{0,k}
is fixed, it suffices to show
\mathbb{E}[\bm{T}_{4,k}]
.
Recall from that
\bm{T}_{4,k}=\sum_{j=1}^{55}\bm{S}_{4,j,k}
(see
Section
F.1.4
). We start with the group
j=1,\ldots,20
.

Terms for
j=1,\ldots,4
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,1,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}

\displaystyle=-\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})(d-r_{k})\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,2,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,3,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,4,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{0}.

Terms for
j=5,\ldots,16
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,5,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,6,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})(d-r_{k})\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,7,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,8,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,9,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,10,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,11,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})(n-r+1)\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,12,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,13,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,14,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,15,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,16,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{0}.

Terms for
j=17,\ldots,20
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,17,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}

\displaystyle=-\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,18,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}

\displaystyle=-\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})r_{k}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,19,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}

\displaystyle=-\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,20,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}=\bm{0}.

It turns out that for any
j=1,\ldots,20
.
\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,j,k}=c\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

for some scalar
c
. Then applying (
79
), we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{S}_{4,j,k}+\bm{S}_{4,j+35,k}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,j,k}+\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,j+35,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,j,k}+\mathbb{E}-\bm{S}_{4,j,k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle=\bm{0},

where the last equality holds since
\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,j,k}=c\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}
.
This implies that

\sum_{j=1}^{20}\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,j,k}+\sum_{j=36}^{55}\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,j,k}=\bm{0}.

We continue with
j=21,\ldots,35
.

Terms for
j=21,\ldots,25
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,21,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,22,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,23,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,24,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,25,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})(d-r_{k})\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

Terms for
j=26,\ldots,29
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,26,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=0

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,27,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=0

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,28,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=0

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,29,k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})(d-r_{k})\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

Terms for
j=30,\ldots,35
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,30,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,31,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle=-\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})(d-r_{k})\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,32,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,33,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,34,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,35,k}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{P}_{U}^{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{U_{k}}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{W}^{\perp}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}\bm{E}_{k}^{\top}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}=\bm{0}.

In summary, we have that

\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{4,k}=\sum_{j=1}^{55}\mathbb{E}\bm{S}_{4,j,k}=\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})(d-r_{k})\bm{U}_{k}^{\star}\bm{W}^{\star\top}.

Then

\mathbb{E}\bm{T}_{0,k}\bm{T}_{4,k}^{\top}=\sigma^{4}(n-r-r_{k})(d-r_{k})\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{W}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k}^{\star\top}.
